- **1915.04** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/neighbor-carries` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  Anna Moser的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Klara坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Anna Moser把桶一放，夹起Klara进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Katharina Bauer赶来时，Klara正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (膝盖擦破，已冲洗); food; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1915.12** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1916.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1916.06** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; flag +scandal_misdirected; knowledge “被登出来的签名是书记员的，更正启事在第七版。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `r13-fam.repair-friendship::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/argument-reopened` tags: task, social, friendship, repair, trust, vienna, urban, crisis
  话说到一半，Rosa Winter又把三年前的旧账翻出来，声音越来越高。Klara把杯子放下，没有还嘴，只把桌上的水渍擦干。Rosa Winter忽然停下来，问Klara是不是觉得自己有理；Klara说不是，只是不想再摔第二个杯子。
  _effects: mood -0.02000000000000035; condition; flag +friendship_truce; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000909:child_

- **1939.09** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-government/sold-the-share` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Anna Weiss把Klara寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Klara追到码头时，Anna Weiss已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Klara在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。
  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000260:child; flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition; mood -0.07999999999999996_

- **1939.09** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/quiet-solidarity` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Klara没有发言，只把带来的面包分成几份。散会时，一个不认识的女人把她的地址塞给Klara，说那条街还需要有人继续收会费。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1939.10** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home, injury
  Klara托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Katharina Bauer擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Klara晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Katharina Bauer的腿伤有人料理，Klara却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (看护妇料理后伤势好转); health -0.019999999999999997 (夜间加班的疲劳)_

  Lena带了两只空箱子来，走的时候还是空的。Klara把三年的合同、收据和会议记录按月份排在地上，Lena抽查了十二份，每一份都能对上签字人；最后一句是“下次可以少准备一点”。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; organization_funds; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +passed_audit; knowledge “审计抽查了十二份合同，每一份都能对上签字人。”_

- **1940.01** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara正要把空床许给Anna Weiss，Josef却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Klara回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Anna Weiss，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000260:child_

- **1940.02** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.first-come-order` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara把四只洗衣盆按到厨房的先后排成一列，用粉笔在门后写下各家的时辰：六点到七点半、七点半到九点。Katharina Lechner起初嘟囔，看到自家名字排在第二格，也就把水壶提了回去。晾衣绳按同样次序分两段，谁家的床单先上谁先占。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000558:child_

- **1940.05** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Klara当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  _effects: reputation 0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008456_

  第二轮投票是十七比十七。按章程，主席可以投第二票；Klara把自己的名字念了两遍，会议室里没有人笑。散会后，Franz Kral在门口说下一次会带更多的人来，Klara说椅子够用。
  _effects: reputation -0.02; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; flag +tie_broken_by_chair; knowledge “章程允许主席在平票时投第二票，但没有人记得上一次使用是什么时候。”_

- **1941.01** `curse15-bargain.borrowed-name::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-government/the-candle-bites` tags: wish, power, curse, ritual, bargain, office, irony, vienna
  仪式做完，Franz Kral把铜钥匙放进Klara手里，钥匙却烫得像刚从炉子里夹出来。Klara松手时掌心已经起泡；第二天，聘书上的名字褪成一片空白，只有Klara的手还疼。Franz Kral说这算成了，也算没成。
  _effects: state_add curse-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:195; flag +curse_recoiled_at_once; cash -1 (烫伤的药膏（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +curse_bargain_made_

  Klara把空煤筐拎到隔壁门口，说只借一筐，等手头松了就还。Rosa Huber看了看筐底的锈，进屋铲了半筐，又从灶边拿了两块引火的柴。Klara道谢时，Rosa Huber说这条走廊里借东西要记着，不是记在纸上。
  _effects: food; condition; flag +borrowed_coal_from_neighbour; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; flag +kept_district_list_private; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Luise Winter 2点了牛奶咖啡，Klara要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡（其中0.12克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。

  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:203; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); flag +hired_as_festival_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个); health -0.010000000000000002 (在风口里走了一段，脸被吹得发红)_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.04999999999999999; condition; flag +saw_patrol_beat_an_innocent_man; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; knowledge “the old ragpicker was beaten for carrying a hook”_

  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; reputation -0.12; cash -5 (moving and lost rent（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

  新设的统一登记处把营业执照、税务登记和卫生许可并成一张表。Gustav Lechner把表推过来，说一次填完，三个部门共享。Klara填到“营业范围”时，Gustav Lechner说现在可以写“综合零售”，不必逐项列；Klara写了，心里却想着烟草和热食以后还查不查。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-148; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-148_

  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000705:child_

  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; flag +rival_audited; knowledge “委员会的差旅账目里有两次重复报销。”; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: cash -2 (疾病保险的印花（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +enrolled_in_sickness_insurance; role; knowledge “sickness benefit pays half wages for the first six weeks”_

- **1952.02** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/turn-away-with-fare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara直说家里腾不出地方，从抽屉里摸出半克朗车费和一块黑面包，塞给Elisabeth Hofer。Elisabeth Hofer的藤箱提手已经磨毛，在门槛上磕了一下。Klara关上门，听见脚步声沿楼梯往下，看门人在楼下问了一句，Elisabeth Hofer答得含含糊糊；厨房里少了一块面包，也少了一个本该住下的人。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (给表亲车费（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004498:child; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/term-ends-clean` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  任期在十二月三十一日结束。Klara把印章擦干净，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把；Emil来接手续，两个人把最后一份移交清单对了两遍。走出大门时，门房照旧叫了一声“先生”，没有加头衔。
  _effects: position_end 临时主席 @org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:006643; reputation 0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +left_office_in_order; knowledge “移交清单上最后一项是印章，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1953.01** `r13-civ.canvass-election::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/won-a-few-votes` tags: politics, election, canvassing, street, party, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara夹着一叠印着候选人头像的传单，从巷口走到巷尾。有人在围裙上擦手，问候选人是不是也住这样的楼。Klara答不上来，只把传单往门缝里又塞了一张。天黑时，口袋里只剩三张。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +canvassed_election; role; skill_

- **1953.03** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/small-surplus` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  账目连续第二年没有超支，Gustav Berg 2在报告里加了一句“未发现异常”。Klara用结余给夜班守卫换了炉子，又把旧账本按年份装进箱子；没有人写报道，但来办事的人发现窗口开得比从前早。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.06; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; flag +quiet_surplus; knowledge “审计报告里只有一句“未发现异常”，但窗口提前开门被办事的人记住了。”_

- **1953.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.far` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Klara顺着河岸一直走，走过铁路桥又走回来，天已经黑透。回来时脚底发热，袜子磨在脚背上，她一路数着路灯走完最后一段巷子，进门先坐下脱了鞋。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1953.10** `curse15-bargain.borrowed-name::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/a-key-and-a-title` tags: wish, power, curse, ritual, bargain, office, irony, vienna
  办事处后门的Emil把一份旧名册翻到空页，让Klara在“会长”后面签下名字。三天后，Klara真的收到一枚铜钥匙和一张聘书：夜校同学会会长，职责是保管储物间。同学会只有三个成员，储物间的锁已经坏了。Emil没有再出现。
  _effects: organization_found 夜校同学会 (other); position 夜校同学会会长 @org:curse15-borrowed:evt:world-vienna-1912:015096; state_add curse-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:332; reputation 0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (聘书与印花的费用（其中1克朗记入债务）)_

- **1953.10** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015096_

- **1954.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/clean-audit` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Gustav Berg 2带了两只空箱子来，走的时候还是空的。Klara把三年的合同、收据和会议记录按月份排在地上，Gustav Berg 2抽查了十二份，每一份都能对上签字人；最后一句是“下次可以少准备一点”。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; organization_funds; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “审计抽查了十二份合同，每一份都能对上签字人。”_

- **1954.01** `state_transition:curse-bargain/the-curse-lands` tags: curse-bargain, curse, ritual, wish, bargain, debt, secret, supernatural

  _effects: state_transition curse-bargain -> cursed-by-wish; flag +curse_landed_after_bargain_

- **1954.01** `r13-civ.apply-passport::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/passport-issued` tags: politics, passport, papers, migration, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Berg 2把出生证、居住证明和两张照片排成一列，又让Klara把右手放在登记簿上。印章落下去时，Klara听见隔壁窗口有人在哭。护照是硬纸封面，照片上的Klara没有笑。
  _effects: cash -8 (护照窗口的出生证（其中8克朗记入债务）); flag +passport_issued; knowledge “the passport is valid for two years and must be carried at t”_

  Klara要了一杯水，Gustav Berg 2给了半杯，说喝完再想。窗外的钟走过十四圈，Otto Berg换了三次班，最后把一张释放证明放在桌上。Gustav Berg 2说“回去想想”，Klara走到门口，腿一软，扶着墙才没有跪下。
  _effects: condition; flag +held_without_charge_for_a_day; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; knowledge “the police can hold a person for a day without a charge”_

- **1955.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/motion-carries` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  投票结果是十九比十七。Klara把印章、钥匙和一本写满批注的账本放进纸箱，最后一圈走廊走得比平时慢。Lena在门口等着接手，手里已经拿着新的编制表；表上第一个被划掉的名字是Klara的秘书。
  _effects: position_end 名誉主席 @org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:006643; reputation -0.050000000000000044; mood -0.07000000000000006; flag +removed_by_vote; knowledge “新编制表第一个划掉的是我的秘书。”_

  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1955.04** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/mother-returns-to-work` tags: health, family, home, injury
  Katharina Bauer不肯躺着，第三天就去帮人洗衣挣回一克朗。Klara晚上发现她的腿又肿了，那点工钱抵不上药钱；炉边的药布还没干，又得重新煮。
  _effects: health -0.05000000000000002 (过早劳作导致腿伤加重); cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱)_

- **1955.11** `r2-uni.censor-proof::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/tipped-the-censor` tags: media, censorship, proofs, newspaper, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Berg 2问第二套清样在哪里，Klara说在印刷所。Gustav Berg 2走后，Klara给Lena递了张纸条，让Lena把清样烧掉。Lena没有烧，而是把纸条交给了警察。第二天，Klara的办公桌被撬开，账册不见了。
  _effects: cash 5 (第二套清样); flag +tipped_censor_about_proof; knowledge “the printer kept the second proof and informed the police”_

- **1955.11** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-chain-only` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  链子从你指缝里滑下去，落在绒布上几乎没有声音。掌柜说两克朗四十赫勒，你点了点头。出门时你把表掏出来上了几圈弦，它还在走，只是再没法挂在马甲上了。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得)_

- **1956.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016514_

- **1956.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/signed-for-a-favor` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Gustav Berg 2在签字前把一张剧院包厢的票根留在桌上，说是“给孩子的”。Klara把票根推回去，却在许可证上签了名；排水图仍缺一页，但窗口当天就盖了章。扩建开工后，第一场雨把后院淹了半尺。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; flag +permit_for_favor; knowledge “许可证少了一页排水图，盖章时没有补。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.borrow-handcart/hire-the-movers` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  站在门口把借钱的话过了两遍，最后没有开口。转身去找了街角的搬运行，一辆板车加两个帮工，一趟一克朗五十赫勒。家里能当的都当了，现钱只够这一趟，搬完当天连买煤的钱都要现算。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (搬运行一趟的工钱（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-174; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-174_

- **1956.05** `r2-law.jury-duty::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/holdout-juror` tags: law, jury, court, verdict, civic, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton Berg把十二个人领进小屋，门一关，Klara就说证据里那把刀没有指纹。其他人想快点了事，Klara把法条抄在纸上，一行行念。念到第三个小时，有人开始敲桌子；最后陪审团没能达成一致，Gustav Berg 2宣布重审。
  _effects: cash -3 (陪审团里的第十二个人（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +hung_jury_on_missing_fingerprints; role; knowledge “the murder weapon was never fingerprinted”_

- **1956.06** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Klara手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94_

  Gustav Berg 2把汇款单退回来，说收款村的地址在新区划里改了名，钱只能退回。Klara又填了一张，把旧村名和新村名并排写上。Gustav Berg 2说这样不行，系统只认一个名字。汇款单在柜台上放了一下午，最后被盖上“无法投递”。
  _effects: cash -1 (汇款单背面的三行字（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +remittance_address_failed; knowledge “the post office accepts only one official village name”_

  Klara说货车在道口堵了六分钟，Gustav Berg 2没听完就把一张印好的书面警告拍在工具柜上，说这个月第二次就交到人事那里。Klara把纸条折进内袋，回机床边站定；Lena在门口摇了摇头，把剩下的半个黑面包掰了一块塞过来。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; food_

- **1959.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/sinecure-created` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Klara在编制表最下面添了一行“顾问”，月薪两克朗，职责是“必要时提供咨询”。Franz Hofer第二天就搬来一张靠窗的桌子，带来一盆天竺葵；一个月后，Katharina Lechner发现顾问唯一经手的文件是自己的午餐菜单。
  _effects: position 顾问 @org:curse15-borrowed:evt:world-vienna-1912:015096; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:008290:child; knowledge “顾问岗位没有固定职责，考勤由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1959.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.give-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Klara只喝了半杯就看见门口站着等位的老主顾，手里转着帽子。Klara把报纸叠好放回木架，起身让出窗边的位子，一杯 Melange 三十赫勒，侍者点头致意，没再收第二杯的钱。走出门时雨小了些，Klara站在檐下理了理衣领，觉得这半个下午也不算白来。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (一杯 Melange 的价钱); reputation 0.030000000000000027; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-185_

- **1959.03** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-chain-only` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  链子从你指缝里滑下去，落在绒布上几乎没有声音。掌柜说两克朗四十赫勒，你点了点头。出门时你把表掏出来上了几圈弦，它还在走，只是再没法挂在马甲上了。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1959.06** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Klara点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Katharina Lechner量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1959.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Rosa Weiss 2攥着Klara的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

- **1960.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019893_

- **1960.01** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/the-list-was-already-signed` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  上一任在离任前签了一份四十人的任命名单，最后一行没有日期。Klara把Emil Lechner 5的名字添在第四十一行，注明“补录”；发薪日，会计问这四十一个人里为什么有六个已经去世。
  _effects: position 编外顾问 @org:curse15-borrowed:evt:world-vienna-1912:015096; flag +forty_first_name; knowledge “上一任留下的四十人名单里，有六个已经去世。”; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-191_

  _effects: historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1959 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015000000000000013_

- **1960.04** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1960.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1960.08** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Hermann Moser 2用膝盖顶住，Klara把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销（其中1克朗记入债务）)_

- **1960.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺结核中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1960.11** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara数出八赫勒交给Gustav Weiss 2，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Gustav Weiss 2说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Klara盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120; flag +funeral_society_member; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120_

  报纸把Klara称作“新任主席”，照片很大，办公室很漂亮。真正的决定在另一间屋里做出。Klara每天签字，签的都是别人写好的东西。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation 0.07999999999999996; position 名誉主席 @org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:010971_

  Maria 2请Klara去家里坐一夜，说只要Klara在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Klara收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Maria 2把面粉分给Klara一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006508:child; flag +sold_mourning_service; condition_

  Klara在邮局填了一张六克朗的汇款单，Gustav Berg 2扣了手续费，说那边只能收到五克朗二十分。Klara在背面写：买煤，别省着吃。三周后Josef Bauer的回信到了，说煤买了，剩下的钱给邻居家的孩子交了学费。
  _effects: cash -6 (汇款单背面的三行字（其中3克朗记入债务）); mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +sent_remittance; relation friend with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “the post office deducts a fee before the money leaves the co”_

- **1961.06** `r2-law.legal-aid::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government::r2-law.legal-aid-reform/clinic-loses-file` tags: law, legal_aid, poverty, court, bureaucracy, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara第二次去时，Gustav Berg 2找不到上次的登记卡。柜子里堆着三个月的表格，Gustav Berg 2翻了半小时，说可能送到了区里。Anton Berg记得Klara的名字，却记不得欠条金额；Klara把数字写在手上，伸过去给Anton Berg看。
  _effects: condition; flag +legal_aid_file_lost; knowledge “the clinic lost the intake card between visits”_

- **1961.08** `r3-ind.time-clock::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/four-minutes-docked` tags: work, office, time_clock, wages, discipline, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara跑进厂门时，打卡钟刚跳过七点零四分。Anton Berg把卡片抽出来，用红笔在边上画了一道，说这个月已经第三次。Klara解释电车在桥上停了，Gustav Berg 2从车间门口喊，说迟到就是迟到，别把桥上的事带进厂里。
  _effects: cash -3 (迟到四分钟（其中3克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +docked_for_late_arrival; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; knowledge “the third late mark costs half a day’s pay”_

- **1962.01** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-government/term-ends-clean` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  任期在十二月三十一日结束。Klara把印章擦干净，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把；Katharina Lechner来接手续，两个人把最后一份移交清单对了两遍。走出大门时，门房照旧叫了一声“先生”，没有加头衔。
  _effects: position_end 临时主席 @org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:010971; reputation 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “移交清单上最后一项是印章，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把。”_

- **1962.01** `p10-migration-tasks.pawn-belongings/pawn-watch-and-coat` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, pawn, sale
  他把怀表和两件冬衣递上铁栅栏后的柜台，当铺的人翻过表壳、抖了抖衣领，报出九克朗，写在一张蓝纸当票上。他留下那套木工工具，把当票折成小方块塞进身份证后面，数钱时手指有点抖。
  _effects: cash 9 (当掉怀表与两件冬衣换来的现钱); knowledge “当铺赎当期限六个月，过期死当，蓝纸当票要收好”_

- **1962.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Klara把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +union_ledger_public_

### 120 subject events

- **1914.06** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Alois Lechner提热水壶往木盆里兑水时壶底脱了焊，滚水溅在左手背上。她咬着牙把壶搁下，Mitzi被响声惊得大哭。手背起了水泡，接下来三天她只能用一只手洗尿布。
  _effects: health -0.08000000000000002 (左手被开水烫伤); mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Mitzi的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Mitzi在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Katharina Lechner哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Mitzi蹲下把Katharina Lechner驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Katharina Lechner听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Mitzi的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Alois Lechner回来接过孩子，Mitzi扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: health -0.03 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000558:child_

- **1919.03** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/vinegar-compress` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  碗里倒上醋，兑一半凉水，Alois Lechner把布巾浸透，拧到不滴水，敷在Mitzi的额头上。布一热就换，换下来的搭在床沿。Mitzi半夜说梦话，Alois Lechner用手背试了试温度，又去换一盆水。天亮时枕布湿了一片，Alois Lechner的手指白得像泡过石灰。
  _effects: health 0.03 (湿敷后退了烧); mood 0.02; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Mitzi把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Mitzi踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Mitzi单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000004 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1920.06** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-scarlet` tags: childhood, street, social, home, injury
  巷子里一家接一家地病倒。Mitzi烧到说胡话，Alois Lechner把阁楼小屋腾出来，在门把手上系了块红布。医生隔着门看了Mitzi的喉咙，留下药粉，收下一克朗二十赫勒。Alois Lechner把家里留着买煤的钱数了两遍，刚好够。
  _effects: health -0.2 (猩红热); cash -1.2 (付医生诊费); condition_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Katharina Lechner的鞋掉在井台边，Mitzi先去捡鞋，回头才看见Katharina Lechner摔在湿石头上。Otto Winter用围裙裹住Katharina Lechner，血从膝盖渗出来。Alois Lechner夜里把Mitzi叫到灶台前，数落了很久，最后把锅里剩下的半勺汤倒回大锅，只给Mitzi留了半碗。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.06; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1921.02** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Mitzi跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Hedwig Stein从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: health -0.04000000000000001 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-hedwig-stein_

- **1921.04** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1921.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1921.09** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Rosa Weiss家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Mitzi递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Rosa Weiss端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Mitzi把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1922.08** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场里的菜摊一字排开，Mitzi把十五克鲁泽排在掌心，先买了土豆，又去盐摊上要了一小包。摊主用旧报纸把盐裹好，Mitzi把它塞进围裙内袋，土豆兜在身前。回家时Alois Lechner接过铜板一枚枚数，土豆一颗没少。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Mitzi把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Mitzi踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Mitzi单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1923.04** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书在Mitzi的书包里藏了三天，课间才敢翻两页。还书那天，Mitzi把书角淋皱的事先说了，又把书页一张张抚平。Anna Huber翻到最后一页，看了看书脊，说下次别带进院子，书却还是借给了Mitzi。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +wish:knowledge:pursuing_

- **1924.08** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne捏了捏Mitzi的虎口，又让Mitzi张开手指，说骨节还没长开，推硬木会伤手。Alois Lechner想再问一句，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne已经转身去搬木料。Mitzi退到门槛外，听见刨子重新响起来，木花从门缝里卷到脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.08; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  客户刚把床单接过去，Maria Moser就从楼梯口上来，盯着Mitzi看了两秒，说“你不是这家的”。Maria Moser当场把工钱收回，说明天要去找Anna Lechner的雇主。Mitzi站在门口，手里还攥着空筐绳，回家一路上没吃东西，Anna Lechner问起来，只摇了摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000410:child; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1925.09** `r1-child.reform-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/half-a-basket-of-coal` tags: reform_school, theft, institution, poverty, punishment, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi从煤场后墙翻进去，装了半筐碎煤。巡夜人抓住她的领子，送到区公所。Gustav Berg说念在初犯，送感化院半年。Alois Lechner在表格上按手印时，手指抖得按了三次。煤筐在雪地上拖出一道黑印，一直拖到区公所门口。
  _effects: condition; flag +sent_to_reform_school; role; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; move place-wien-magistrat_

- **1926.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-government/acquaintance-in-office` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi的邻居在市政厅当差，替Mitzi把材料直接送到教育科。三天后，转学证寄到家里，上面多了一行手写批注：“准予插入三年级。”Mitzi把证翻来覆去看，发现自己的名字被写错了一个字母。名字写错的事Mitzi没去改，反正学籍号是对的，老师点名时也这么叫。
  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved; knowledge “the school transfer certificate misspells the name”_

- **1926.02** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/go-to-brickworks` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social, injury
  “下周一，跟街尾的工头走。”Alois Lechner说完就把算术本推到桌角。Mitzi知道再争也没用。Anna Huber在门口拦住Mitzi，把一张夜校地址塞过来，说晚上可以去。Mitzi把纸条折进鞋垫，第二天就去了砖厂。头一周四克朗，搬砖搬得腰直不起来，纸条在鞋里磨得发毛。
  _effects: role; cash 4 ({subject}砖厂首周工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (砖厂头几天搬砖); flag +has-evening-school-address; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

  Grete Stein替Mitzi填了“父亡”，说这样就不用查工会记录。Mitzi看见Alois Lechner晚上在灯下补那双送报纸的鞋，鞋底磨得只剩一层布。放榜时名字在第七位，Mitzi没有告诉家里表格上写了什么。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +lied_on_scholarship_form; relation friend with person-grete-stein; knowledge “the scholarship form lists the father as dead”; education secondary_

  _effects: reputation -0.039999999999999994; flag +police_caution_recorded; relation guardian with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the police opened a caution file in the subject’s name”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1927.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/factory-hand` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment, injury
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把Mitzi分到装订间，每天站十个小时，工钱按件算。第一周结束，Mitzi的指关节肿了，工头说习惯就好。
  _effects: employment Buchbinderhilfe @ org-winter-print; health -0.040000000000000036 (standing all day); condition_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/delivery-paid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Mitzi背着床单筐走过两条街，肩膀被带子磨出一道红印。第二户人家的女佣数了件数，把工钱用纸包好。Mitzi回家时Katharina Lechner还躺着，Mitzi把纸包塞到枕头底下，没叫醒人。晚饭时Katharina Lechner自己坐起来端碗，Mitzi才觉得肩膀没那么沉。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (替妹妹送床单的计件工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (背着湿床单走了几个街区); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000558:child; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1927.09** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Mitzi把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Alois Lechner端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Mitzi那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/joins-union` tags: adolescence, labour, union
  工会的人在厂门口拦住Mitzi，说学徒工也该有工票和保险。Mitzi每月交一克朗会费，把会员证塞进上衣内袋。
  _effects: role; cash -1 (union dues); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999_

  Mitzi跟着Rosa走进印刷厂后面的屋子，交了十赫勒会费，在借书簿上按了手印。他挑了一本讲蒸汽机的册子，封面烫金已经磨掉；管理员说下月十赫勒要按时交，书才能续借。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.04000000000000001_

  为赶选举传单，Mitzi连排三个晚上。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把加班算成“学徒练习”，只多给半克朗。Friedrich Moser 2在楼梯间说这是常事，叫Mitzi周末去八角亭替人抄货单。
  _effects: cash 0.5 (三晚加班只补半克朗); health -0.050000000000000044 (连续三晚排字熬到十点); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1928.10** `vienna-youth-threshold.first-wage/half-for-shoes` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  鞋铺橱窗里一双结实的工作鞋标价四克朗二十赫勒，Mitzi的鞋底已经能吸进雪水。回家后Mitzi把信封拆开，一半放在桌上，一半揣进内袋，说下个月不向家里要鞋钱。Alois Lechner没说话，只把桌上的钱一张张抹平。
  _effects: cash 4 (工资的一半交给家里); cash -4.2 (买一双不漏水的工作鞋); health 0.050000000000000044 (脚不再整日泡在湿冷里); relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

  Mitzi还是把这件事告诉了母亲。母亲没多问，在厨房案板旁多摆了一副碗筷，晚上让Rosa睡在炉边的旧垫子上，说先住下，别的事明早再想。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; food; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/damp-linen-returned` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  客户把床单贴在脸上，又摸了摸边角，说“还是潮的”，把门合上一半。Mitzi把床单卷回筐里，背回洗衣房。Katharina Hofer没骂，只让在后院重晾，件钱等重送完再结。Mitzi蹲在地上翻布角，晚风吹得膝盖发酸，天黑前才夹完最后一只夹子。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (蹲久了膝盖发酸，又吹了晚风); mood -0.03999999999999998; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1929.05** `r2-leisure.ferris-wheel::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/wheel-restarted` tags: leisure, fairground, ferris_wheel, fear, youth, rural, agriculture, small_town
  摩天轮在最高处停了七分钟。Hermann Weiss把纸袋里的栗子分给Mitzi，说反正下面的人也上不来。Mitzi看见整个游乐场的煤气灯排成一圈，河在更远处发黑。Katharina Hofer在下面喊，说齿轮卡了，别站起来。话音刚落，座舱晃了一下，开始往下走。
  _effects: mood 0.08000000000000002; flag +stuck_on_ferris_wheel; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000264:child; knowledge “the wheel gear sticks when the crowd is heavy”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

  最后一道弯，Mitzi没有捏刹车，借来的车架在石子路上跳了两下。Alois Huber的黄色车轮就在右边，链条发出干涩的响声。Mitzi先冲过线，Franz Kral举起旗子，Emil Weiss在人群里喊，别摔了我的车！Mitzi把车停下来，才发现车把上全是汗。

  成绩单上差两分。Friedrich Lechner说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Alois Lechner来签字。Alois Lechner在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Mitzi把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

- **1929.12** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/enroll-night-class` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  Rosa Winter 2在黑板上写下课程表和书本费：一学期三克朗，另加六十赫勒杂费。Mitzi下工后啃着冷土豆赶到教室，坐到公共长桌最靠煤气灯的一头。手指上还留着白天的油污，握笔时在本子上蹭出灰印，但座位号已经登记在册。
  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费); education secondary; skill; health -0.030000000000000027 (白天上工夜里上课睡眠不足); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Friedrich Lechner的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

  _effects: cash -2 (申请材料与抄写费用); mood -0.04999999999999999; reputation 0.03; skill_

- **1930.10** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Maria Hofer把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Mitzi把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Maria Hofer低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.020000000000000018 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1931.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.request-raise/request-raise.granted-extra-duty` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, raise, task:work
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把怀表扣在桌面上，一刻钟走得清清楚楚。Mitzi把那张纸推过去，一行行念，念到替人顶班时停了一下。厂长说加一克朗，钥匙和月底盘点也归你。
  _effects: cash 4 (加薪一周一克朗的积攒); skill; relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1931.08** `vienna-printshop-daily.overtime-order/overtime-refuse` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  Mitzi把围裙挂回钉子，走过Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne身边时两人都没说话。第二天字盘上摊着整版六号小字，眼睛要贴着看。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; mood 0.03999999999999998; skill_

- **1931.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/refuses_grudge` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton Weiss敲门想借半块煤给发烧的孩子烧热水，Mitzi那天刚和Anton Weiss为走廊堆东西吵过嘴，没开门，隔着门板说自家也不够用。后半夜听见Anton Weiss抱着孩子下楼去敲药房的门。第二天在走廊碰见，Anton Weiss低头绕过去，手里的铜壶碰在栏杆上，响了一声。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1931.09** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Rosa Huber用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Mitzi才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; flag +parish_registered_

- **1932.01** `vienna-printshop-daily.wage-shortfall/wage-take-it` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  Mitzi把少了钱的工钱袋塞进内袋，没再问。回家路上买了两块面包，比平时少一块。
  _effects: cash -1.8 (工钱被扣); food; mood -0.06_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Mitzi在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Mitzi的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Josef的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

- **1932.01** `task-vienna-activist.recruit-coworker/recruit-coworker-reported` tags: politics, activism, union, party, leaflet, meeting, task, task:politics
  下午刚开工，工头就把他叫到成品堆后面，说有人反映他在食堂拉人入会。工头没提名字，只让他把手里那批活计先放下，去仓库清点废纸。年轻工友整个下午都避着他的目光。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; flag +recruitment_reported_to_foreman_

- **1932.04** `r1-work.construction-scaffold::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/fell-to-the-brick-pile` tags: work, construction, scaffold, fall, wind, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi踩的那块跳板一头没有绑牢，风一吹就翘起来。Mitzi从第二层摔到砖堆上，Luise Weiss扔下灰桶跑过来。Ferdinand Weiss先去看跳板，再来看人，工地郎中摸了摸骨头，说手腕断了，得去医院打石膏，今天的工钱照扣。
  _effects: cash -8 (风从脚手架背后过来); health -0.3 (风从脚手架背后过来); condition; flag +fell_from_scaffold; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1932.05** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi数出八赫勒交给Otto Winter，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Otto Winter说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Mitzi盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1933.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Josef在几天后找到Mitzi，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-depression/laid-off` tags: adulthood, depression, work, unemployment
  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Mitzi的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-divorce/separate-under-one-roof` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation
  Mitzi和Josef把床铺分开，米和煤也各算各的。两人仍住在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，因为谁都没有钱再租一间房；孩子在两床之间来回，三克朗买了隔开的锅碗和煤油，家里从此有两本账，夜里两盏灯先后熄灭。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.08000000000000002; mood -0.07999999999999996; cash -3 (separate household goods)_

- **1933.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-ambulance` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  助理医生拿不准，叫了救护车。Mitzi被抬进医院，走廊里等了六个小时才排到床位；家里人后来算了一笔账，救护车比药贵。救护车在石板上颠，他抓着担架边，数着路灯。医院门口的担架已经排到街上。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; cash -15 (ambulance and hospital admission); health 0.050000000000000044 (admitted for observation and oxygen); role; knowledge “实习医生拿不准就会叫救护车；走廊等六个小时才有床，车费比药贵。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1933.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.post-ledger` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Mitzi把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +union_ledger_public; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1933.08** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Alois Huber说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Mitzi站在原地看着Alois Huber拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; flag +uncertain_courtship_

- **1934.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.wait-for-the-brother` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Gustav Winter把话说得很慢：母亲还在，弟弟的学徒期还有三年。Mitzi点头，把准备好的结婚证词折起来放回口袋，那是借教堂的旧纸写的。两人在电车站分开，约好明年复活节再谈。Gustav Winter上车前替母亲拢了拢披肩，没有回头看。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000395:child; flag +engagement_postponed; cash -1 (tram fare to the meeting)_

  _effects: flag +learned_proper_bandaging; skill; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000256:child_

  _effects: cash -1 (病房里的糖水梨); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +hospital_visit_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; knowledge “the friend asked only for cold water”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +ordinary_sad_year_

- **1934.09** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Allgemeines Krankenhaus病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1934.11** `r3-crime.body-identification::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital::trainload-of-wounded/the-tag-was-switched` tags: crime, death, morgue, family, war, vienna, urban, crisis

  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +identified_the_wrong_body; knowledge “the morgue tags were switched before the identification”_

- **1934.12** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  _effects: cash -4 (夜里敲接生婆的门); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +witnessed_stillbirth; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000410:child; knowledge “the midwife said the cord had been around the neck”_

### 220 subject events

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Josef Huber在绳子那头喊。Josef踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Josef Huber数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Josef把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Josef Bauer把Josef拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Josef把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Josef蹲下把两包并排，按Josef Bauer说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1919.05** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Josef被推到拱廊柱子边，围裙带子绕了两圈，系在铁环上。Josef一挣，肩膀撞上石柱，整条胳膊麻了一下。Hedwig提着菜篮过来，把篮子往地上一放，从怀里摸出削土豆的小刀，挑开绳结，没跟那两个孩子说一句话。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.05; condition_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Josef拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Anna Moser弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Josef接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser_

- **1920.05** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Josef正低头看卡上的皇帝胡子，一个穿背带裤的男孩从侧面撞过来。卡飞出去，落在马车辙里。Josef扑过去捡，手掌被碎石划开一道口子。Mitzi追到巷子尽头，只抓下一块袖布。回来时Josef还蹲在地上，把沾泥的卡往袖子上擦。
  _effects: mood -0.08; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; condition_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Josef前面的大婶一直往后靠，Josef就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Josef的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1921.12** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/old-shoes` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  报纸窸窸窣窣打开，露出一双旧鞋。Josef认出鞋帮上的补丁，是弟妹穿剩下的。Josef把脚伸进去，脚后跟还空着一指，脚趾却挤在前头。Katharina Bauer看了看，说垫层布就好了，起身去柜子里翻布头。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; health -0.020000000000000018 (鞋挤脚走路发疼)_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Hermann Kral家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Josef递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Hermann Kral端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Josef把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; skill_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  井台边的石沿湿得发亮，Luise Huber一挣，Josef只能把Luise Huber抱离地面。院子里晒的床单还没收，Josef背着Luise Huber在布影里来回走，用一块碎布给Luise Huber擦鼻涕。等Luise Huber睡沉，Josef的肩窝已经压出一道红痕，端碗时手指直抖。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000685:child_

- **1923.10** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Josef每天放学都在柜台前站一会儿，看玻璃板底下压着的烟画，顺手把歪了的报纸摞齐。Klara擦完柜台，从抽屉最里面摸出一张揉皱的风景卡，边角还沾着烟丝，塞过来时说了句“别挡着门”。Josef把卡夹进课本，第二天带了一张重复的来给Klara看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1924.01** `task-vienna-youth-apprenticeship-search/trial-promised` tags: task, task:work, work, adolescence, apprenticeship

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; flag +apprenticeship_trial_promised; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1925.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/tears-on-the-page` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Luise Huber把铅笔芯写断了两次，第三遍还是算错，眼泪滴在本子上，把墨迹洇开。Josef把本子拿开，说再写一遍，孩子却把椅子往后一推。母亲在灶边喊别吵，Josef只好把豆子收进碗里，听见Luise Huber在床上抽鼻子。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +homework_ended_in_tears; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000685:child; condition_

- **1925.03** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Wohnhaus Victor Adler, Gumpendorfer Straße 54 / Blümelgasse 1病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1925.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1925.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1926.01** `r13-civ.first-aid-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/course-cancelled` tags: health, first_aid, course, skill, school, reform, medical, schooling

  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +first_aid_course_cancelled; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  _effects: reputation -0.04; flag +police_caution_recorded; relation guardian with person-katharina-bauer; knowledge “the police opened a caution file in the subject’s name”_

  校长在名册上把Josef的名字划到低一年级，说缺的课不会自己长回来。Friedrich Moser 2已经比她高半个头，课间在走廊里喊她的旧绰号。Josef把书包带子绕在手腕上，绕了三圈。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +held_back_after_dropout; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1926.12** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Josef站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Elisabeth Weiss 2追出来说座位可以保留两周，Josef点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.04000000000000001_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Josef下周一到排字房报到，先搬铅字和扫地，月薪十八克朗。家里用第一笔预付工资买了双结实的鞋。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance); role_

- **1927.01** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Katharina Hofer先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Josef每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03999999999999998; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:46; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; education apprenticeship_

- **1927.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-to-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Josef在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Josef把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。
  _effects: flag +boarding_school_admitted; education secondary_

  Berta Lechner说入伙要交二十个铜板买蜡烛，Josef掏遍口袋只有九个。他们让他在楼梯口等，等到天黑也没人下来。楼上传来笑声，像隔着一层厚被子。Josef把九个铜板在楼梯上排成一排，又一个个收进口袋。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +excluded_from_friend_group; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1930.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.buy-for-elder` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Josef替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Josef自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。

  隔壁Anna Bauer的小儿子烧到说胡话，额头烫得能烘手。Anna Bauer在楼道里抹眼泪，说请不起医生。Josef下楼叫了街角药房的伙计，花四十赫勒买了一包退烧药粉，又帮着用湿布敷了一整夜。天亮时孩子退了烧，Anna Bauer端来一碗热汤面，面底下卧着一个鸡蛋。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person-anna-bauer_

- **1942.07** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef和Grete Hofer谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Josef家，钥匙只在白天给。Grete Hofer说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Berta Kral听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Josef把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.09** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household, injury
  Josef把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Josef的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart); health -0.020000000000000018 (moving in winter); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1942.12** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/serve-first-talk-later` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef先给Luise Winter盛了满满一碗，扁豆和面团结结实实压在勺底，那块熏肉切开分了两份，锅里的吃食折合两克朗。药费的事一句没提，只问要不要再添点汤。Luise Winter吃到一半才开口，说上次说话太冲；桌边多摆的那把椅子，最后有人坐下了。
  _effects: food; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  Josef开口借钱读夜校，Luise Huber坐在新买的沙发上，说家里也有难处，孩子要上学。Josef看见茶几上放着一盒没拆的进口糖，没有提。临走时，Luise Huber送到门口，说以后少来往，省得两边都为难。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +sibling_refused_help; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000685:child_

  _effects: cash 1 (检举邻居得到的配给奖励); reputation 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; flag +denounced_neighbor_for_grain; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

  _effects: move place-wien; cash -4 (边境小屋的住宿和补办许可); flag +border_refused_then_admitted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; condition; health -0.030000000000000027 (硬板床和等待); knowledge “入境许可过期可以先等审查，但行李在边境小屋不安全”_

  Grete Hofer把绳子往木桩上绕第三圈时，Josef走过去解开了结。Ferdinand Berg的脸已经肿了，嘴里塞着一只袜子。Anna Weiss赶来时，人群还没有散。Grete Hofer说“你护着Ferdinand Berg，明天丢的就是你家”，Josef把绳子扔在地上，说“那就丢”。
  _effects: condition; flag +opposed_vigilante_justice; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; knowledge “the suspect was tied up behind the mill without evidence”_

  Josef拎着鱼上楼，看见Anton Berg蹲在门口用刀刮锅底。Josef把鱼放在两家之间的窗台上，切了一半推过去。第二天门口多了一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

  _effects: flag +relative_listed_missing; role; knowledge “a missing declaration delays the pension”_

  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

  介绍时把两边都夸过了头，说老友是印厂里最靠得住的人，说女工的手艺整条街都找不出第二个。话太多，两人反倒拘谨起来，各自看窗外。咖啡凉了，坐了不到一个钟头就散了，出门时谁也没提再见面。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和点心); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

  Berta Kral说三号院的老太太咳得直不起腰，Josef把一个木箱底的碎煤单独拢出来，用报纸包好。借条上写的是两千克朗，其实只值几赫勒的煤，但手印按得很重。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  掌柜的木尺敲了两下柜台，声音短促。Josef把问题又问了一遍，掌柜才说师兄是“老规矩进来的”。师兄这时转过身，笑了一下，笑得不太自然，说“没事，你问得对”。
  _effects: reputation -0.04000000000000001; skill_

  Berta Stein早在Josef上任前就挂在一个“临时调查员”的名下，工资由上一任签字。Josef不能新设岗位，只能补一份追认函；函上写明“工作内容另行通知”，而通知一直没有发出。
  _effects: organization_funds; flag +retroactive_sinecure; knowledge “临时调查员的岗位是上一任留下的，工作内容从未书面通知。”; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

  名单上添的是Friedrich Stein 2，一个连公文格式都分不清的远房亲戚。Emil把考勤表放在桌上，没有问原因；Josef也没有解释，只在签字栏里写得比平时慢。第一个月，Friedrich Stein 2迟到了七次，工资照发。

  Grete Huber在门口站了很久，说只要一张床，不问别的。Josef看见楼下停着一辆挂军牌的自行车，没敢开灯，把Grete Huber领进厨房，挪开碗柜，后面有一块能放下草垫的凹墙。Alois Lechner第二天来收租，Josef说碗柜后面在修水管；那天夜里，Grete Huber第一次睡满四个小时。
  _effects: cash -4 (阁楼上的第三张床); food; flag +hid_in_kitchen_recess; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003509:child_

  _effects: state_add strike-committee state:world-vienna-1912:229; mood -0.020000000000000018; knowledge “厂主答应下周谈，但车间门口已经多了一道新的考勤栏。”_

  窗外有人来问入会，接待的人一时不知道怎么开口，Josef替他说“进来坐”。老成员把账本拿回去，用一块旧布包好，放进抽屉最里面。抽屉关上的声音比平时响。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; flag +union_debt_written_off_

  Josef把托盘稳住，还是有一圈咖啡沿着瓷碟淌到Anna Lechner的袖口。Anna Lechner站起来，丝绸裙摆上已经是一块深色。Josef道歉，掏出今天的零钱，k. u. k. Landwehr-Infanterie, Wiener Ergänzungsbezirk在旁边说，干洗费从工钱里扣。Anna Lechner看了看那几枚硬币，只拿走了一枚。

  _effects: cash -5 (居留章盖在旧地址上); flag +residency_permit_renewed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; knowledge “a landlord’s confirmation can correct the address on a resid”_

  _effects: cash 2 (广场上的空椅子); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +sold_bag_made_from_dead_husbands_uniform; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

  _effects: employment Kurzarbeiter @ org-winter-print; role; cash -4 (每周少上两天班); condition; flag +short_time; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120_

- **1948.09** `r3-war.mia-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-mia-clarification/found-unidentified-burial-record` tags: war, missing, bureaucracy, family, grief, vienna, urban, postwar
  战后清理档案，Emil找到一张模糊的埋葬记录，上面写着“无名，步兵，1916年7月”。Josef问是不是自己的兄弟，Emil说不能确定；Franz Berg建议去公墓看编号。那天下午，Josef在编号前站到关门。
  _effects: flag +found_unidentified_burial_record; role; knowledge “an unknown infantryman was buried in July 1916”_

- **1949.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/waiting-list` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  Josef去住房局问结果，办事员翻过卡片说分数不够：家里人口少，现住房虽挤但还没到拆除标准。回程的电车上，Anton Weiss 2劝他明年再递一次申请。
  _effects: role; cash -2 (application and tram fare); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1949.01** `r13-edu.study-group::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/group-dissolves` tags: education, study_group, mutual_aid, politics, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef的读书会坚持了两个月，Klara找到正式工作后再没来过。Josef一个人坐在阁楼里，把两个人的笔记对了一遍，发现自己漏掉的内容比记住的还多，就把Klara那半本也抄了下来。抄完以后，Josef把两本笔记都锁进箱子。锁扣坏了。
  _effects: skill_

- **1949.08** `r13-rel.neighborly-help::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::neighborly-help-war/hid-the-extra-ration` tags: relationship, neighbor, help, obligation, illness, vienna, urban, postwar
  巡警来查配给证时，Anna Lechner把Josef多领的一份藏进自己的篮子。Josef事后要还，Anna Lechner说等打完仗再算。两人把面包分着吃了，谁也没提那张证是谁的。
  _effects: flag +shared_ration_risk; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000410:child; knowledge “the neighbor hid an extra ration during an inspection”_

- **1949.12** `r2-faith.shiva-leave::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sat-full-week` tags: faith, mourning, shiva, employer, dismissal, vienna, urban, postwar
  出殡后的第二天，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让人送来一张纸条：丧假只算三天，第四天不到就算自动离职。Josef把纸条压在烛台下，坐满七天。第八天去上工时，门口的名牌已经换了人。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +observed_full_shiva_and_lost_job; knowledge “the employer counts mourning days as unauthorized absence”; unemployment (dismissed for observing the seven days of mourning); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1949.12** `r2-leisure.lottery-ticket::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/lost-the-lottery` tags: leisure, lottery, hope, poverty, chance, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef把彩票摊在桌上，对着报纸上的号码看了三遍。前两个数字对上了，第三个差一位。Josef Moser说至少中了一个数字，Josef说一个数字不值钱。Helene Moser在门外说，上一期的头奖到现在还没人领。
  _effects: cash -2 (夹在祈祷书里的彩票); mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +lost_lottery; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `r13-work.apply-loan::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/loan-granted` tags: commerce, credit, bank, guarantor, capital, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena把申请单看了两遍，问Josef拿什么抵押。Josef Bauer把自己的房契复印件放在柜台上，说这条街上的铺子不能倒。Lena让两人分别按了指印，批了八十克朗，分十二个月还。出门时Josef把钞票缝进内袋，针脚走得很密。
  _effects: cash 25 (银行柜台前的担保人); flag +work13_obtained_a_bank_loan; relation friend with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “the loan is repayable over twelve months at five percent”_

- **1950.02** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-army/sold-the-share` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Luise Kral把Josef寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Josef追到码头时，Luise Kral已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Josef在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。
  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱); relation rival with person-luise-kral; flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition_

- **1950.03** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Rosa Weiss用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Josef才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; flag +parish_registered_

- **1950.04** `r3-mig.remittance-failure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/returned-address-unknown` tags: migration, remittance, family, failure, money, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Lechner把汇款单和一张退条一起推过来，退条上盖着“地址不明”。Josef说上个月还寄到过，Gustav Lechner说“上个月是上个月”。五克朗扣了两克朗手续费，退回来的钱只够买三天的面包。
  _effects: cash -5 (退回来的汇款单); condition; flag +remittance_returned_address_unknown; knowledge “returned remittances lose the fee twice”_

- **1950.07** `r1-fam.household-merging::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/merging.refused` tags: family, household, fire, overcrowding, mutual_aid, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef没有开门。Berta Huber在门外站了一夜，第二天去了救济站；Josef从窗户里看见他们排队，把窗帘拉上了。三个月后，Berta Huber在市场上遇见Josef，点了个头；Josef回家，把厨房里多出来的那张草垫卷起来，塞进了柜子顶。
  _effects: flag +refused_to_merge_households; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1951.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Josef在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Josef拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Anton Huber 2找到Josef时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1951.04** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Alois Huber合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1951.10** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Friedrich Berg 2凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Josef把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1951.12** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013749_

- **1951.12** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Hermann Moser却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Josef把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1952.01** `task-vienna-family-care.room-move/move-north-room` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Josef和房东把租金讲定，每月少三克朗，窗户自己修。Alois Huber搭手搬箱子，Lena帮着把玻璃裁好装上；省下的钱买了煤、一包铁钉和一扇旧窗框。孩子们争了半晚上，最后按个头排了铺位，朝北的墙上还渗着冷气。
  _effects: residence; cash -2.5 (买玻璃、铁钉和煤); mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1952.09** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Josef对Otto 2说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Otto 2把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Josef的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005257:child_

- **1952.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.notary-inheritance/demand-accounts-first` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  旧账本摊开，纸页上的铅笔字迹深浅不一。Anna Bauer念一笔，Josef就在边上记一笔，Klara的钢笔悬在遗嘱上方没有落。Katharina Bauer咳了一声，说屋顶那八克朗是自己看着修的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; flag +demands_estate_accounting_

- **1952.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Alois Lechner在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Alois Lechner敲开Josef的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1952.10** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/take-in-a-lodger` tags: housing, home, health, family, injury
  Josef把厨房角落隔出一块，租给一个在附近上工的学徒。每月多四克朗，但煤、水和厕所都要排队；夜里有人咳嗽，Josef睡不踏实，第二天眼下发青，孩子也在床上翻来覆去，四克朗换来一屋子醒着的人。
  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); health -0.040000000000000036 (overcrowded housing); condition_

- **1953.01** `r13-civ.help-refugee::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sheltered-two-nights` tags: social, refugee, shelter, war, trust, vienna, urban, postwar
  Helene Moser站在走廊尽头，脚边一只用绳子捆住的纸箱，问能不能借宿两晚。Josef把厨房的折叠床打开，又烧了一壶水。夜里，Helene Moser在梦里说了一种他听不懂的话，Josef把门闩又检查了一遍。
  _effects: cash -2 (走廊里的陌生口音); food; flag +sheltered_refugee_two_nights; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1953.03** `r2-law.pawn-ticket::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/redeemed-before-sale` tags: finance, pawnshop, debt, household, loss, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton Berg 2把当票对着账本看了两遍，说昨天刚过期，今天还能赎，但要加一个月的保管费。Josef把工钱和借来的钱一起推过柜台，Anton Berg 2从后架取下那只银表，表盖上的划痕还在。Berta Stein在门口说，再晚一天就上拍卖台了。
  _effects: cash -12 (过期的当票); flag +redeemed_pawned_watch_before_auction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124; knowledge “the pawnbroker charges one month’s storage after the ticket ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-124_

  Josef没有立刻答话，先去敲Rosa 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Rosa 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Josef把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003215:child; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

  教堂的洗礼簿被水泡过，Josef出生那一页只剩半个日期。Lena说要有两个证人，Josef Bauer当场举手，说孩子是她在家生的，接生婆已经死了。Lena让她按手印，又让Josef把日期念一遍，写进新的证明里。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +birth_record_reconstructed; relation friend with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “a sworn statement can replace a damaged baptism entry”_

  Josef把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Josef折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。

  印泥是赭红色的，像干了的面包瓤。Josef按完手印，工头把表举到窗边吹干，阳光里那张纸透出指印的轮廓。楼下第十区的街面传来运煤车的铁皮声，星期六下午突然显得很宽。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

- **1954.09** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Karl Kraus敲了门。Josef掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Karl Kraus什么也没说就走了。夜里Josef听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1954.11** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family

  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152_

- **1955.01** `p10-family-tasks.appeal-inheritance/appeal-inheritance.court` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, appeal
  兄弟把信推回来，说笔迹不对，办事处的人把清单合上，让两人去法院。Josef在楼下问过律师，诉讼费三十克朗。她把信按在胸口站了一会儿，楼梯间的灯一闪一闪，兄弟已经走到街对面。
  _effects: mood -0.07000000000000006; relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “遗产诉讼费要三十克朗，办事处不受理私人纠纷”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-168_

- **1955.06** `r1-cul.painting-commission::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/repainted-the-hand` tags: culture, painting, patronage, class, desire, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Kral看了三天，最后用指节敲了敲画布上那只手，说太像我的手，但不像我。Josef把右手重新上色，留了一道没干的铅白；Ferdinand 2在屏风后换衣服，衣架碰倒了一只瓷瓶。Josef Kral没有回头，只说下个月再来。
  _effects: cash 8 (肖像上的那只手); flag +commission_accepted_with_revisions; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; knowledge “the patron wants an idealized hand, not a likeness”_

- **1956.01** `task-vienna-family-care.care-sick-parent/night-watch` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Josef整夜没合眼，按时把最后两包药粉喂给Josef Bauer，水盆换到第五次。天快亮时咳嗽声稀了，老人的呼吸慢慢平下来。Josef在椅子上打了个盹，醒来时炉子灭了，赶到工坊迟了一刻钟，被扣了半天工钱。

### 226 subject events

- **1916.02** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Wohnung Karl Kraus, Lothringerstraße 6病倒了，痢疾让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1916.03** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从痢疾中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1916.11** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/old-shoes` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  鞋底补过两层，针脚很粗，鞋头磨得发白。Rosa把脚塞进去，脚趾顶到前头，只能蜷起来。Alois Lechner问紧不紧，Rosa说正好。Alois Lechner蹲下按了按鞋帮，说明年脚再长就放不下了，把针线盒从桌上挪开。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; health -0.020000000000000018 (鞋挤脚走路发疼)_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Anna Moser从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Rosa。Rosa把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1918.03** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  医院走廊里全是湿鞋印。Alois Lechner把Rosa放在长椅上，自己去窗口交钱，六克朗换来一张薄薄的收据。护士把Rosa领进观察室，白灯照得人睁不开眼。Rosa抓着Alois Lechner的袖口不放，直到药水推进胳膊才松手。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票); health 0.08 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.039999999999999994_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Anna Lechner的鞋掉在井台边，Rosa先去捡鞋，回头才看见Anna Lechner摔在湿石头上。Emil Weiss用围裙裹住Anna Lechner，血从膝盖渗出来。Alois Lechner夜里把Rosa叫到灶台前，数落了很久，最后把锅里剩下的半勺汤倒回大锅，只给Rosa留了半碗。
  _effects: health -0.03 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.06; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Anton Kral把Rosa拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Rosa把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Rosa蹲下把两包并排，按Anton Kral说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Rosa站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Katharina Lechner睡醒哭了一声，Rosa把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Alois Lechner回来时，Rosa正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000558:child; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1926.04** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/go-to-brickworks` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social, injury
  “下周一，跟街尾的工头走。”Alois Lechner说完就把算术本推到桌角。Rosa知道再争也没用。Rosa Winter 2在门口拦住Rosa，把一张夜校地址塞过来，说晚上可以去。Rosa把纸条折进鞋垫，第二天就去了砖厂。头一周四克朗，搬砖搬得腰直不起来，纸条在鞋里磨得发毛。
  _effects: role; cash 4 ({subject}砖厂首周工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (砖厂头几天搬砖); flag +has-evening-school-address; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费); education secondary; skill; health -0.030000000000000027 (白天上工夜里上课睡眠不足); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

  _effects: household; role; role; cash -8 (ring, moving costs and a small engagement meal)_
