  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004071_

- **1934.01** `r13-edu.engineering-study::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-school/bridge-holds-thirty-five-kilos` tags: education, engineering, university, model, calculation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton用木条和铁丝搭了一座一米长的桥，加载到二十公斤时，中间开始下弯。Rosa Winter 2让Anton再加一根斜撑，第二次加载到三十五公斤才断。Anton把断口收进盒子，毕业多年还留着，木条上写着当天的日期。盒子后来被Anton的孩子翻出来当玩具。
  _effects: flag +engineering_diploma; skill; education higher_

- **1934.08** `r3-crime.stolen-bicycle::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory::long-commute/chain-snaps-at-the-gate` tags: crime, transport, police, work, fraud, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton骑着那辆车去上工，链子在大门口断了。Anton Berg蹲下来帮忙，看见车架上没磨干净的编号，站起来拍了拍手上的油，说厂里丢过一辆同样的车。中午，Friedrich Berg 2在门房等着Anton，车就靠在墙边。
  _effects: cash -4 (车架上新刷的蓝漆); condition; flag +bicycle_traced_to_factory; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; unemployment (suspended after a stolen bicycle was found at the factory gate)_

- **1934.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton请楼下的Anna Berg 3白天来照看Josefine Hofer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Anna Berg 3上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Anton下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Josefine Hofer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; health 0.039999999999999994 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗)_

- **1934.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Anton下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1935.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.neighbor` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  老太太接过孩子，用旧围裙兜着坐在窗边，说一克朗一周，六点送来六点接。Anton数出硬币，把奶瓶放在她灶台边。第三夜孩子还是哭醒，Anton抱着他在屋里走，天亮前靠着床沿打了个盹。
  _effects: cash -1 (每周付邻居老太太的照看费); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1935.05** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  汤锅冒着热气，Anton盛出两勺倒进Karl Kraus带来的罐子里。Karl Kraus说了声谢谢就匆匆上楼。隔天罐子还回来，锅底搁着一小块猪油。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1936.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::pharmacy-war-requisition/pharmacy-war-morphine-trade` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, crisis
  军需处先领走了奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林和泻盐。Anton用一双旧靴子换了两支吗啡，药剂师把安瓿裹在报纸里，叮嘱别在街上打开。他把安瓿藏进帽衬，过检查站时低着头。
  _effects: cash -20 (boots and cash traded for two morphine ampoules); health 0.019999999999999997 (pain relief from a black-market ampoule); role; flag +traded_for_morphine; knowledge “军需处先领走奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林；旧靴子可以换两支吗啡。”_

- **1937.01** `r13-turn.leaving-faith::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/formal-apostasy` tags: faith, religion, apostasy, family, conflict, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton在堂区办公室递了一张纸，写明不再参加圣事、不再缴纳教堂税。Ferdinand Winter看了两遍，问是不是因为葬礼上的事。Anton说不是，是不再信了。Ferdinand Winter把名字从名册上划掉，笔尖在纸上停了一下。出门时，家里有人正坐在教堂门口的石阶上等。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; flag +formally_left_the_church; role; relation rival with person-ferdinand-winter_

- **1937.01** `p9-history-union-political.reform-meeting/reform-read-petition` tags: politics, union, party, meeting, leaflet, police, strike, reform
  提问时间一到，Anton站起来，把请愿书上的三条逐条读出：房租上限、成年男子普选、十小时工时。Franz Kral在台上让记录员一条条记下，礼堂里有人跟着低声重复那三个数目。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; knowledge “市政厅旁的会议上，官员让记录员把三条请愿要求记进了正式记录”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1937.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Anton的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Anton被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call); health 0.03 (examined by a teaching team); knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”_

- **1937.11** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.wire-fix` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Anton把钟背板卸下来，发现一根发条断了。她从缝纫盒里找出一截细铁丝，把断头缠住，钟摆晃了几下又走了起来。走是走，每天慢一刻钟，她索性把指针往前拨了拨，凑合着看。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1938.01** `r14-edu.scholarship-collector::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/teach-it-off` tags: education, scholarship_trial, debt, reunion, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Franz Kral在校门口等到天黑，手里拎着一只旧公文包。包里是当年补助金的收据和一张夜校课表。Franz Kral说学校缺一个不要工钱的识字班教师，问Anton还不还这笔账。Anton接过课表，纸边被汗浸软，第二天晚上就站到了黑板前。讲义钱两克朗由Anton自己出。
  _effects: cash -2 (多年以后来收账的人); flag +repaid_scholarship_with_teaching; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004430_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/flees` tags: politics, persecution, 1938, migration
  Anton在夜里收拾了一只箱子，把证件缝进外套内衬，搭上往西的火车。Klara第二天来查人，只看到空床和没关严的窗。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -20 (escape and lodging); condition_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton把Luise Moser的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Luise Moser今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Anton听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 15 -> 23; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 47; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton把Ferdinand Moser的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Ferdinand Moser今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Anton听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton把Alois Moser的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Alois Moser今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Anton听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `r13-civ.quarantine::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::r13-civ.quarantine-crisis/crisis-quarantine-work` tags: health, quarantine, epidemic, neighbor, confinement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton把派工单从门缝底下捡起来，上面写着“疫后复工，逾期除名”。他把白线擦掉半截，走到街口又停住，因为岗亭里坐着人。回家后，Anton把派工单钉在墙上，像钉一张欠条。
  _effects: cash -1 (门框上的石灰线); mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +quarantine_job_threatened; knowledge “the employer threatens to strike quarantined workers from th”_

- **1938.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Emil Weiss在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Emil Weiss敲开Anton的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.05** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Emil Lechner把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Anton留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Anton在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods); mood -0.06000000000000005; role_

- **1938.06** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household
  Anton把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Anton的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1939.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Anton和Friedrich Lechner 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Friedrich Lechner 2在门后放了一根木棍，Anton把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton被叫到学校，老师说Anton Moser这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Anton没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/dismissed-as-nerves` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton把夜里咳醒的次数写在一张包装纸上，Josef Huber扫了一眼，说是心事太重，给了几包安神粉。走廊尽头的护士压低声音，说住院部还有空床，但要医生签字。Anton把包装纸折回去，没再问。
  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); mood -0.07000000000000017; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1939.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Anton把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; skill_

- **1939.07** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/quiet-solidarity` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Anton没有发言，只把带来的面包分成几份。散会时，一个不认识的女人把她的地址塞给Anton，说那条街还需要有人继续收会费。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000395:child_

- **1939.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Berta Huber一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Berta Huber的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1940.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Berta Huber抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Anton把床单对折，腾出一半，Berta Huber用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007499_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton被叫到学校，老师说Luise Moser这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Anton没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 18; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 47 -> 52; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils)_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton被叫到学校，老师说Ferdinand Moser这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Anton没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。

  Anton被叫到学校，老师说Alois Moser这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Anton没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 23; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 47; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils)_

- **1940.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.first-winery` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Anton和Katharina Bauer在第一个酒棚就坐下了，要了一壶新酒，Emil Hofer摸出一副磨损的纸牌。三人打到下午，太阳偏西也没再往上爬；Anton输了几个铜板，连酒带牌局一共一克朗五十赫勒，倒赢回一肚子笑话。回程的电车上，Anton靠着窗，酒意上来，差点坐过站。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (酒棚的新酒和牌局输的铜板); relation friend with person-katharina-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

- **1940.05** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Katharina Bauer把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Katharina Bauer在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1940.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Anton胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙)_

- **1941.01** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Friedrich Berg 2把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Anton把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Friedrich Berg 2低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; flag +rent_increase_accepted_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton被叫到学校，老师说Anton Moser这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Anton没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 15 -> 10; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 55; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1941.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/neighbor-soup` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton Berg端来一碗兑了水的牛奶，顺手把Anton家的炉子捅旺。Anton靠在床头，听Anton Berg讲楼下谁也被传染了，谁家把病人送去了医院。人走后，屋里还留着一点煤烟和奶味。
  _effects: cash -1 (退烧后的第一碗粥); health 0.04 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1941.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Ferdinand Moser这周主动擦了黑板。Anton把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 26; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008722_

- **1941.04** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-deposit` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household, injury

- **1941.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Anton靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912_

- **1941.12** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.buy` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼摊快收了，Anton挑了一条最小的鳟鱼，摊主顺手抓了把欧芹丢进纸里。黄油在锅里化开，鱼皮卷起来，楼道里都是味。Josef Kral敲门时，Anton从鱼尾切下一小块递过去。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鳟鱼和黄油); food; mood 0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1942.01** `statistical_death/other` tags: death
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang突发急病，高热和虚弱持续了几天。医生没能给出明确诊断，Anton在一个夜里去世。Luise Moser在场。
  _effects: death (急性疾病)_

### 221 subject events

- **1915.12** `statistical_infection`
  Hedwig在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1916.01** `statistical_recovery`
  Hedwig从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1917.12** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  铁环在石板缝上颠了一下，Hedwig用钩子一拨，抢先绕过煤车。Marie Huber跟在后面，铁环倒在地上转了两圈。三颗玻璃弹珠从Marie Huber的口袋里摸出来，有一颗还带着体温。Marie Huber把弹珠按进Hedwig手心，转身踢了一脚墙根。
  _effects: relation friend with person-marie-huber; mood 0.05; skill_

- **1918.01** `vienna-life-school-enrolment/enrolled` tags: childhood, school, fees

  _effects: education primary; cash -2 (school books（其中2克朗记入债务）); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

  _effects: mood -0.04; food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  _effects: cash -1 (医生的诊金（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.06 (服药后退烧); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1925.09** `r14-edu.entrance-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/short-by-two-points` tags: education, exam_failure, entrance_exam, shame, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Hedwig在放榜墙前从头看到尾，没有找到自己的名字。差两分。Otto Hofer把Hedwig叫到办公室，说作文写得太满，结尾却空了。Luise Weiss的名字在第二行，用红笔圈着。Hedwig回家后把课本码齐，最上面一本压着没写完的志愿表。补考费一克朗，Hedwig没有去交。

  Otto Hofer让全班按座位排队，Josef Bauer在讲台边摆开酒精瓶和针盒。轮到Hedwig时，Otto Hofer替她把袖子卷好，说别怕，去年他自己也种过。下课后，黑板上还留着一行没擦掉的日期。

  游乐场新装了紧急制动，铁牌上写着“由市议会验讫”。摩天轮升到一半，Otto Winter拉了一下闸，座舱猛地停住，又慢慢往下走。Hedwig问这是不是坏了，Otto Winter说这是新规矩，每趟都要试一次。Gustav Winter在座位上把栗子撒了一地。
  _effects: mood -0.03; condition; flag +tested_new_ferris_brake; relation colleague with person-otto-winter; knowledge “the municipal inspector requires a brake test on every ride”_

  _effects: mood -0.12; condition; flag +escaped_reform_school_

  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +rejected_mentorship; skill_

  Hedwig请楼下的Anton Kral白天来照看Josefine Hofer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Anton Kral上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Hedwig下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Josefine Hofer的嘴唇不再干裂。

  厂里说半天工没了就没了，Hedwig没再争。厨房里少了一只面包，晚饭只有一锅糊糊。伴侣把勺子放下，说来年春天再说。夜里Hedwig替邻居补袜子，三个赫勒一双，缝到灯芯烧短。
  _effects: cash -4 (辞掉半天工少了一份收入（其中4克朗记入债务）)_

- **1939.03** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig和Josef Bauer一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Hedwig去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Hedwig先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1939.12** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/give-place-to-mother` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Rosa抱着孩子连声道谢时，Hedwig已经弯着腰在咳。药房玻璃瓶上贴着褪色标签，喝下去满嘴甘草味；后半夜Hedwig听见自己牙齿打颤，炉子早灭了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (街角药房买退烧药（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1940.01** `state_expire:under-investigation` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick, state:under-investigation
  Hedwig的「被调查」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:155_

- **1940.01** `task-vienna-family-care.ask-relative-money/ask-for-weekend-work` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  钱字到了嘴边，Hedwig改了口，问Josef Huber铺子里要不要一个周末帮工，搬货、上架、跑腿都行。Josef Huber上下打量他一番，说周六早上六点来，一天一克朗，干不好就不留。Hedwig应下，回家路上算了算，要干满五个周末才够房租。
  _effects: cash 1 (周末帮工的工钱); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; flag +weekend_shop_work_

- **1940.04** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-pot-empty` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  锅底刮出焦味，Lena说今天就这些了，明天赶早。Hedwig把空罐子夹在腋下往回走，石板路上结了薄冰，Luise Kral 3在拐角等着，二话没说把罐子递过来。
  _effects: food_

- **1941.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.box-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Hedwig咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Hedwig手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Maria Hofer等在门口，问值不值，Hedwig只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.020000000000000018; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1941.07** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.share-spinach` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  那把菠菜根上还带着湿泥，叶子被压得有些蔫。Anton先松了手，说你家人口少，拿半把够了。Hedwig把菜装进篮子时，听见摊主开始收秤砣，铁盘碰得叮当响。
  _effects: cash -0.04 (分买最后一把菠菜); food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1941.11** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.labour-exchange/exchange-trial-work` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, labour_exchange, bureaucracy
  洗衣房里蒸汽弥漫，熨斗压在衬衫领子上咝咝作响。老板娘翻了翻Hedwig熨好的第一件，说领口还差些火候，手上倒还稳。两打熨完，三个克朗放在围裙口袋里，铜板还带着体温。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

- **1942.01** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig没有立刻答话，先去敲Alois Stein 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Alois Stein 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Hedwig把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1942.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.read-in-aisle` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Hedwig站在书架前抽出一本厚小说，就着门口的光读了半章，Klara在柜台后抬起眼皮看了一眼，没说话。读到要紧处，Hedwig摸出铅笔头把页码记在车票背面，把书插回原处，说下次带够押金再来；白读半章，管理员的眼神却凉了一点。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1942.04** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +watch_pawned_

- **1942.08** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼还滴着水，Hedwig在走廊里分成两份。一份拿进自家，一份搁在Friedrich Lechner 2的门口。傍晚Friedrich Lechner 2来还盘子，盘子里是一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

- **1943.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.relative-introduction/relative-introduction.never-mentions` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, relative, introduction
  点心纸包搁在膝上，Luise Kral 2说熟人多，慢慢问，别急。Hedwig回家把这些话学给母亲听，母亲只叹了口气。那包糖后来自己家里分着吃了。
  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; cash -1 (再买一包糖上门（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1943.09** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.caretaker-notice` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Hedwig去找看门人，说院子里四面墙，声音绕不出去。第二天楼梯间的木板上贴了一张纸：晚上十点后请勿放留声机，鞋匠铺也请收锤。纸条没有落款，只有房东事务所的戳。Anna Huber路过时看了很久，把留声机的盖子合上了。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1943.09** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-granted` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Hedwig在圣文生会门口登记，领到一张面包券和一小袋土豆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.10** `vienna-market-debt.coal-credit/coal-credit-full` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  邻居帮你把半筐煤抬上楼，楼梯窄，煤块滚了两级。你蹲下去捡，指甲缝里立刻嵌了黑泥。炉子生起来后，屋里那股潮冷气慢慢退了，你才想起晚饭还没着落。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (赊煤到期还款含利息（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); food; flag +coal_credit_debt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1944.01** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/second-bridge-promise` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  在第二个桥洞下，Anton Weiss 2先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Hedwig点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子（其中0.04克朗记入债务）); mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1944.01** `p10-migration-tasks.find-lodging/lodging-shared-bed` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, lodging, arrival
  他先租了合住的床位，一间屋三张床，和两个工人分摊炉煤。租金最便宜，押金只要两克朗，可夜里有人打呼、有人半夜回来踢到痰盂。他把工具袋压在枕头下，睡了第一夜。
  _effects: cash -3 (合住床位的押金与首周租金（其中3克朗记入债务）); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; knowledge “利奥波德城的合住床位一周约一克朗，押金两克朗”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1944.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Gustav Bauer一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Gustav Bauer的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1944.05** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Anton Weiss 2在楼梯间坐了很久；Hedwig在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Hedwig的名字；此后邻居提起Hedwig，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); role_

- **1944.09** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/shelter` tags: war, homefront, shortage
  警报响时Hedwig躲进地下室，和十几户人挤在长凳上。头顶传来闷响，灰尘从梁上落下。警报解除后，街上多了一堆碎瓦。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1945.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Gustav Bauer嫌Hedwig的床单滴水滴到Gustav Bauer家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Hedwig下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Hedwig把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Gustav Bauer那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009936_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Hedwig躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Hedwig去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Hedwig把欠账按日期抄在纸上，先还利息最高的一笔。Josef翻开米铺账本，把红字划掉一行，写“收到八克朗”。为了凑这笔钱，Hedwig把家里一只旧表送进当铺；Alois Huber知道后，把自家的赊账往后挪了一个月。
  _effects: cash -8 (偿还米铺欠账（其中8克朗记入债务）); role; flag +debt_reduced; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; knowledge “米铺把赊账利息记在红字栏，先还红字最多的那笔最划算。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

  Hedwig把Friedrich Lechner按在墙上，没有推。两人喘着气，谁也没有再动；Josef Winter把欠租单撕下来，揉成一团，说再吵下去这楼里就没人住了。第二天，名单又贴了上去。
  _effects: condition; flag +stopped_before_the_stairs; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000397:child; knowledge “the rent list is pasted back the next morning”_

  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

  链子从你指缝里滑下去，落在绒布上几乎没有声音。掌柜说两克朗四十赫勒，你点了点头。出门时你把表掏出来上了几圈弦，它还在走，只是再没法挂在马甲上了。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1954.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/investigation` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, postwar, reform
  Otto Berg来问过一次话。问题围绕Rosa Weiss的病，也围绕Hedwig最近有没有说过什么威胁的话。Hedwig如实回答，Otto Berg把每个名字都写进了本子。
  _effects: state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:350_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014487_

- **1954.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/it-turns-in-the-hand` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Hedwig收了钱，却在灵堂里试着把那个名字指向Rosa Weiss。蜡烛刚点着，Hedwig自己的手先抖起来，火苗顺着袖口爬上去。Rosa Weiss扑灭了火，没有喊人，只把门打开。Hedwig走出去时，听见身后有人在数剩下的蜡烛。
  _effects: reputation -0.07000000000000006; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.severity 0.8999999999999999 -> 0.9999999999999999; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:358_

- **1954.01** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hedwig在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Hedwig去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为接手后的俱乐部的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1954.01** `r13-civ.join-choir::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/voice-rejected` tags: culture, choir, music, community, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Grete Stein听完，说音准还行，但声音太薄，撑不起礼拜天。Hedwig问能不能先站在最后一排，Grete Stein说等下次有空位。回家的路上，Hedwig在无人的巷子里把那首歌唱完。
  _effects: flag +choir_audition_rejected_

- **1954.09** `r1-cul.church-charity::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/refused-without-certificate` tags: faith, charity, church, hunger, rules, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Lechner让Hedwig先查领汤者的洗礼证，没有证的只能领面包。一个男人把证拍在桌上，说自己在战壕里丢的；Hedwig看了看Gustav Lechner，把汤勺放下了。那天剩下的汤被倒进沟里，热气在雪上化出一个小洞。
  _effects: condition; flag +refused_soup_without_certificate; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000705:child; knowledge “the parish requires a baptism certificate for soup”_

- **1955.01** `curse15-aftermath.the-inquiry::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-parish-clears-it` tags: witchcraft, investigation, reputation, rumor, legal, aftermath, vienna, urban
  教区派人来问了两天，最后在登记册上写：无实据。Elisabeth Bauer 3不肯签字，笔被Lena收走了。Hedwig以为事情到此为止，直到发现邻居开始把自家孩子叫回屋里，只因为Hedwig在门口多站了一会儿。
  _effects: state_remove suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:358; reputation 0.030000000000000027; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Hedwig谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。
  _effects: state_parameter under-investigation.progress 10 -> 25_

- **1955.01** `kernel-migrate.refugee-aid::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/civic-refugee-shelter` tags: migration, refugee, missing_relative, care, postwar, vienna, urban, reform
  她把Elisabeth Bauer 3带回自己家，在厨房支了一张行军床。房东第二天就来敲门，说多住一个人要加钱；她付了半个月的煤钱，把Elisabeth Bauer 3的行李藏进床底。房东来查煤表时，她把行军床折起来靠在墙边。
  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; cash -4 (多住一人要补的煤钱和饭钱（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +sheltered_refugee; knowledge “房东按人头收煤钱，多住一个人要提前说”_

- **1955.10** `r13-rel.neighborly-help::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital::neighborly-help-war/place-in-the-queue` tags: relationship, neighbor, help, obligation, illness, vienna, urban, postwar
  配给队从肉铺排到街角，Elisabeth Bauer 3把自己前面的位置让给Hedwig，说家里还有半袋土豆。Hedwig买完面包，回头看见Elisabeth Bauer 3重新排到队尾，围巾上落了一层雪。
  _effects: cash 1 (借来的煤与一只手); role_

- **1956.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/counts-the-interval` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  Hedwig把两次背叛和两次发烧的日期写在同一张纸上，发现中间都隔着十一天。Hedwig没有告诉任何人，只把纸折成很小的一块，塞进缝衣盒的夹层。那天夜里，Hedwig第一次试着在门口撒盐，动作熟练得让自己停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “背叛与发烧之间隔着十一天。”; skill_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Hedwig谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。

  Franz Weiss 3没有看推荐信，只把Hedwig带到车间尽头，指着一台停着的钻床说，钻三个孔就留下。Hedwig对准划线，铁屑卷成一条蓝灰色的弹簧。Franz Weiss 3把工钱袋往柜台上一丢，说从今天算起，别迟到。
  _effects: cash 1 (铁门开了一条缝); flag +work13_hired_after_trial_at_gate; role; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150; employment Bohrer @ org-winter-print; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-150_

- **1956.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  窗外有人来问入会，接待的人一时不知道怎么开口，Hedwig替他说“进来坐”。老成员把账本拿回去，用一块旧布包好，放进抽屉最里面。抽屉关上的声音比平时响。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; flag +union_debt_written_off; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-176_

- **1956.05** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Rosa Weiss用膝盖顶住，Hedwig把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1956.07** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Hedwig把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Anna Bauer，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Anna Bauer点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1957.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Hedwig在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Hedwig的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1957.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/tells-the-priest` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  Hedwig在告解室里把事情说了一遍，没有提名字。神父沉默了很久，只问Hedwig是否愿意把那张纸交出来。Hedwig说没有纸。走出教堂时，Hedwig发现街对面有人一直在等，手里拿着一本新的登记册。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:413; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

  舅父听完摇了摇头，说钱不借，倒可以给他写张条子，介绍一个跑布吕恩的车夫，让他搭便车走。舅母把点心收进橱里，说路上总得带点吃的。他收了条子，坐车夫的车走了两天。
  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; cash -1 (路上给车夫买烟叶的道谢钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-157_

- **1957.11** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/speak-and-organize` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Hedwig在后屋的会议上报出自己街区的名字：谁家缺煤，谁被房东催租，谁知道哪间印刷厂还在招人。接手后的俱乐部把这些事记进一本旧账本，散会时每个人带走一叠更小的纸条。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.11** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sells-the-mourning` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Franz Berg 3请Hedwig去家里坐一夜，说只要Hedwig在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Hedwig收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Franz Berg 3把面粉分给Hedwig一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156_

- **1958.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/denounced` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Katharina Lechner把Hedwig在夜里说过的话整理成三页纸，交到了办事处。纸上没有一句是假的，但每一句都离开了原来的房间。第二天，Hedwig被叫去问话，问话的人已经知道该问什么。Katharina Lechner在走廊里遇见Hedwig，点了头，像只是借过一支笔。
  _effects: state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:428; reputation -0.06000000000000005; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000558:child_

- **1958.01** `state_reaction:suspected-witchcraft/another-name` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Katharina Lechner在指控Hedwig的人面前添了一句话，说Hedwig曾经在夜里念过谁的名字。那句话没有证据，但登记册上又多了一行。Hedwig后来才知道，添这句话的人就是Katharina Lechner。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 20 -> 30; reputation -0.009999999999999787; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000558:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018563_

  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 8 -> 9_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018563_

  Hedwig把家里人叫到厨房，说老人夜里需要人守，不能只靠一个人。有人提议送养老院，Hedwig把费用算了一遍，众人都沉默了。最后定下轮班表：Hedwig守前半夜，其他人守后半夜；表贴在灶边，第一晚就有人没有来。
  _effects: cash -1 (给床上的老人翻个身（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +organized_elder_care_rota; role; skill; knowledge “the family rota already has a missing shift”_

- **1958.10** `r15-org.workshop-closure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/wages-cut` tags: work, factory, organization, unemployment, collective, vienna, urban, postwar
  厂子没有关，但工钱减了三成，工时加了一个钟头。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说这是暂时的。没有人相信“暂时”，但也没有人当场走。
  _effects: cash -2 (车间门口的封条（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_add strike-wave state:world-vienna-1912:429_

- **1958.10** `vienna-social-movement.aftermath/keep-the-list` tags: development, social_movement, organization, consequence
  Hedwig把夜校同学会的名单藏进缝纫机抽屉的夹层。名单上有二十几个名字，其中两个已经搬走，一个被工厂开除。Hedwig没有把最后一行划掉。
  _effects: flag +movement_list_holder; reputation 0.030000000000000027_

- **1958.11** `r3-ind.telegraph-office::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/delivered-after-the-funeral` tags: work, telegraph, office, delay, message, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig在待领筐底下翻到一封被雨水泡软的电报，收报地址是城外的公墓，发报日期是三天前。Hedwig没有等下一班信差，借了站长的自行车，沿湿路骑到公墓。葬礼已经结束，Rosa Weiss蹲在墓碑前，接过那张纸，看了很久才问：“为什么现在才到？”
  _effects: cash -2 (压在筐底的电报（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +delivered_a_telegram_after_the_funeral; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; knowledge “the condolence telegram sat under the counter for three days”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1959.01** `curse15-aftermath.the-inquiry::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/money-closes-the-file` tags: witchcraft, investigation, reputation, rumor, legal, aftermath, vienna, urban
  Katharina Lechner托人带话，说只要Hedwig出钱修好教堂的侧门，证词就可以当作没有写过。Hedwig把钱数了三遍，最后交出去。侧门修好了，Katharina Lechner在门框上刻了一行小字，刻的是Hedwig的名字。
  _effects: cash -3 (修教堂侧门的封口钱（其中3克朗记入债务）); state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 30 -> 15; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +paid_to_close_accusation_

  Hedwig在回家路上按标签吞了第一剂。半夜醒来时，舌头发木，楼梯在眼前分成两层。她扶着墙走到院子里，吐在雪上，第二天才听人说那瓶药本来该给隔壁的老太太。
  _effects: condition; knowledge “the bottle was meant for another patient”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-180; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-165_

  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 25 -> 35; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱（其中0.3克朗记入债务）)_

  Hedwig在加班格上写了名字，Hermann Weiss 3看了Hedwig发青的眼圈，把名字划掉，说机器不能交给一个站不稳的人。车间里没有工会的人替Hedwig说话，Hedwig想争，腿却先软了一下。回家的路上，Hedwig在药铺门口站了很久。
  _effects: health 0.01 (黑板上的空格); flag +work13_overtime_refused_on_health; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-165_

  Hedwig用松节油把桶底的墨调稀，印出来的黑发灰，剧院主顾只收下一半，另一半退回来。Maria Huber没说话，把退单钉在墙上。
  _effects: cash -0.8 (退货扣掉的计件工钱（其中0.8克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.010000000000000009; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94_

- **1959.12** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.hide-defects` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  篮底那两件领子卷得很紧，Hedwig用围裙角把篮沿擦了擦。Ferdinand Winter翻了翻面上几件，拿尺子比了比，没再往下。Maria Kral 3在走廊里问交了多少，Hedwig含混说了句够买面包。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件)_

- **1960.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/calls-it-a-fever` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Hedwig的「被疑巫术」到期；相关安排结束。

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +factory_story_spiked; knowledge “the editor will not run the story without a named source”_

- **1960.05** `vienna-historical-social-movement.relief-network/open-the-books-again` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hedwig把账本摊在面粉袋上，一栏一栏念，念到某笔去向不明时屋里静了。老人把手套戴上又摘下，Gustav Lechner拿回自己那份登记，说先按墙上的数领。雪还在下，门一直开着。
  _effects: reputation 0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1960.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Hedwig嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Hedwig用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006646:child; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1960.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Gustav Lechner把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Hedwig去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状)_

- **1961.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/counts-the-interval` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  Hedwig把两次背叛和两次发烧的日期写在同一张纸上，发现中间都隔着十一天。Hedwig没有告诉任何人，只把纸折成很小的一块，塞进缝衣盒的夹层。那天夜里，Hedwig第一次试着在门口撒盐，动作熟练得让自己停了一下。
  _effects: knowledge “背叛与发烧之间隔着十一天。”; skill_

- **1961.01** `r13-work.collective-bargaining::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop::reform-eight-hour-clause/employer-stalls-on-hours` tags: work, union, bargaining, wages, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hermann Weiss 3说八小时是给官办工厂的，私人厂子按订单算。Hedwig把合同草案带回去，工人们围着看，没人愿意先签名。拖到第三周，厂里招了几个新工人，合同的事就搁在Anna Lechner的抽屉里，和去年的那张放在一起。
  _effects: flag +work13_hours_clause_stalled; knowledge “the employer is hiring around the eight-hour clause”_

- **1961.05** `vienna-printshop-daily.union-pamphlet/pamphlet-refuse` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  Hedwig把传单推回Rosa Winter手里，说这活不能在我这台机上印。Rosa Winter收起纸走了，第二天车间里多了一个不说笑的角落。
  _effects: relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000909:child_

- **1961.10** `r15-org.workshop-closure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/wages-cut` tags: work, factory, organization, unemployment, collective, vienna, urban, postwar
  厂子没有关，但工钱减了三成，工时加了一个钟头。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说这是暂时的。没有人相信“暂时”，但也没有人当场走。
  _effects: cash -2 (车间门口的封条（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_add strike-wave state:world-vienna-1912:487_

- **1962.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/sells-the-mourning` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Rosa Weiss请Hedwig去家里坐一夜，说只要Hedwig在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Hedwig收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Rosa Weiss把面粉分给Hedwig一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1962.01** `r13-work.collective-bargaining::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory::reform-eight-hour-clause/employer-stalls-on-hours` tags: work, union, bargaining, wages, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hermann Weiss 3说八小时是给官办工厂的，私人厂子按订单算。Hedwig把合同草案带回去，工人们围着看，没人愿意先签名。拖到第三周，厂里招了几个新工人，合同的事就搁在Elisabeth Berg的抽屉里，和去年的那张放在一起。
  _effects: knowledge “the employer is hiring around the eight-hour clause”_

- **1962.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Hedwig手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.06 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

- **1962.04** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/quiet-solidarity` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Hedwig没有发言，只把带来的面包分成几份。散会时，一个不认识的女人把她的地址塞给Hedwig，说那条街还需要有人继续收会费。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

- **1962.12** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Berta Lechner把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Berta Lechner在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_
